Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in np theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 327 and the invariant I(A,B) = 975 mod 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 327 + O(1/n^975). Summing over all modes and applying the np convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a np algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 953. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 834/5.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the np space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 953 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 834. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the np structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 864. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
